*Juntong Peng,Qi Chen,Deyuan Qu,Takayuki Shimizu,Yaobin Chen,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为FleetAgent的云端多模态大型语言模型助手，它通过处理紧凑矢量化V2N消息来辅助大规模自动驾驶车队的远程操作。提出了VecFormer作为矢量到嵌入接口，并构建了VecEval数据集以促进系统训练与评估。实验表明，FleetAgent可以显著减少上行链路负载和KV缓存内存使用，同时提高解释质量和降低干预失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决大规模自主车队在罕见故障时依赖远程操作导致的数据传输成本高及远程操作员监控能力有限的问题，提出了一种能够高效处理紧凑结构化信息并提供自然语言响应的新方法。

Method: 开发了FleetAgent，一个基于云的多模态大语言模型助手，用于处理紧凑矢量化车辆到网络的消息；提出了VecFormer，一种带有可微分Top-K上下文选择机制的矢量到嵌入接口；构建了VecEval数据集，该数据集源自nuScenes，包含配对的人工和合成不完美计划以及人工验证的语言标签。

Result: FleetAgent相较于原始图像减少了高达625倍的上行负载，相比原始文本描述减少了16.54倍的KV缓存内存使用。在VecEval上的测试显示，FleetAgent相比Qwen2.5-VL-7B使用语言描述提高了16.8%的Lingo-Judge分数，降低了19.9%的干预失败率。

Conclusion: FleetAgent利用紧凑的结构化V2N消息实现了高效的、可解释的大规模自动驾驶车队远程操作监控。

Abstract: Large-scale autonomous fleets rely on teleoperation to resolve rare failures, yet streaming raw sensor data from many vehicles is costly, and remote operators can only monitor a limited number of vehicles at a time. We introduce FleetAgent, a cloud-hosted multimodal large language model (MLLM) assistant that consumes compact vectorized vehicle-to-network (V2N) messages, such as map elements, detected objects, and the ego planned path. It provides a structured natural-language response (including narration, explanation, and evaluation of the plan and scene), along with an intervention urgency score for operator prioritization. To make structured messages compatible with token-based MLLMs, we propose VecFormer, a vector-to-embedding interface with differentiable top-K context selection that bounds context length and GPU KV-cache growth, enabling more efficient batch processing, which is important under the context of cloud-hosted large-scale fleet management. We also construct VecEval, a nuScenes-derived dataset with paired human and synthetic imperfect plans and human-verified language labels, to facilitate the training and evaluation of our proposed system. Our proposed system can reduce uplink payload by up to 625 times compared with raw images and reduce KV-cache memory by 16.54 times compared with original text descriptions. On VecEval, FleetAgent improves Lingo-Judge score by 16.8% and reduces intervention failure rate by 19.9%, compared with Qwen2.5-VL-7B using language descriptions. These results demonstrate that FleetAgent can utilize compact structured V2N messaging to enable efficient, explainable teleoperation monitoring for autonomous fleets.

</details>


### [36] [Ultra-Fusion: A Resilient Tightly-Coupled Multi-Sensor Fusion SLAM Framework under Sensor Degradation and Spatiotemporal Perturbation for Intelligent Transportation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21223)
*Yihong Tian,Junjie Zhang,Liuyang Li,Deteng Zhang,Yunfei Zuo,Jie Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Ultra-Fusion的紧密耦合多传感器定位框架，通过统一滑动窗口估计器处理异步测量数据，并支持多种传感器融合选项。该框架在长时间和高速运行、传感器退化以及校准扰动的情况下，能够为轮式、腿式及空中平台提供准确的定位结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的定位对于智能交通系统至关重要，但现有的多传感器融合技术在面对传感器性能下降（如光照不足、LiDAR退化、轮子打滑、GNSS信号丢失）及时空校准误差时仍显脆弱。这些问题在城市峡谷、隧道等环境中尤为突出，导致定位漂移，影响路线跟踪、隧道穿越连续性和局部地图对齐。

Method: Ultra-Fusion框架基于一个统一的滑动窗口估计器，它将不同时间戳的异步测量值排序并转换成可选因子，在同一优化窗口内支持WIO、VIO、LIO和LVIO配置，并可选择性地加入轮速计和GNSS数据增强。采用可观测性感知初始化来选定启动模式，根据可靠性调度逐个因素以排除劣质测量值，并在线执行LiDAR-IMU时空校准，以便在运行过程中细化时间偏移量和旋转外部参数。

Result: 通过扩展M3DGR基准测试集并添加模拟轨迹，研究者们评估了超过60个开源SLAM系统在多个场景下的表现。结果显示，Ultra-Fusion在长时运作、高速度条件、传感器性能下降及校准变化情况下，针对轮式、腿式及飞行平台均展现出了竞争性的准确性。

Conclusion: Ultra-Fusion框架显著提高了复杂环境条件下智能交通系统的定位可用性，特别是在地面自主驾驶、校园与仓库移动性以及低空检查应用中。为了促进工业界与学术界的进步，作者承诺将在论文被接受后公开源代码和相关数据集。

Abstract: Reliable localization is essential for intelligent transportation systems (ITS), including autonomous vehicles, quadruped last-mile carriers, and infrastructure-inspection unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). Although tightly-coupled multi-sensor fusion improves accuracy in favorable conditions, deployed systems remain vulnerable to sensor degradation -- poor illumination, LiDAR degeneracy, wheel slippage, and GNSS outage -- and to spatiotemporal calibration errors. These failures are common in urban canyons, tunnels, and high-speed corridors, where localization drift can degrade route tracking, tunnel passage continuity, and local map alignment. This paper presents Ultra-Fusion, a tightly-coupled multi-sensor localization framework based on a unified sliding-window estimator. Asynchronous measurements are timestamp-ordered and converted into optional factors within one optimization window, supporting WIO, VIO, LIO, and LVIO with optional wheel and GNSS augmentation. Observability-aware initialization selects the bootstrap mode, factor-wise reliability scheduling gates degraded measurements, and online LiDAR--IMU spatiotemporal calibration refines temporal offsets and rotational extrinsics during operation. We extend the M3DGR benchmark with simulation trajectories and evaluate more than 60 open-source SLAM systems on M3DGR, M2DGR-Plus, KAIST, GrandTour, and MARS-LVIG. The results show competitive accuracy across wheeled, legged, and aerial platforms under long-duration and high-speed operation, degradation, and calibration perturbation, improving localization availability for road-level autonomy, campus and warehouse mobility, and low-altitude aerial inspection. To benefit the industrial and academic community, we will release source code and datasets upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [37] [A Human-Inspired Thumb-Index Robotic Hand with Strain Gauges Embedded in Soft Joints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21245)
*Jonas Papenbrock,Shubhan Patni,Tomaso Lisini Baldi,Michele Guerri,Elia Landi,Ada Fort,Matej Hoffmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受生物力学原理启发，开发了Safe Thumb-Index Robotic (STIR) Hand，它是一个轻便、低成本的两指原型，具有不对称的拇指-食指配置。通过将欠驱动的肌腱驱动机械设计与嵌入硅胶封装软关节中的柔性应变计相结合，系统实现了被动抓握适应性，并建立了内部本体感觉和外部感知。数据分析表明，该手在物体分类任务中表现出高精度，证明了传感器化顺应关节显著提高了整体灵敏度并确保了安全抓握，且无需额外的指尖触觉元件或外部视觉系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在通过模仿人类手部抓握适应主要依赖于物理结构和生物反馈之间的协同作用这一生物力学原理，来开发一种既能实现被动抓握适应又具备内外感知能力的机器人手。

Method: 开发了一种名为STIR Hand的两指原型，采用了欠驱动的肌腱驱动设计以及嵌入到柔软关节内的柔性应变计。基于此设计，研究人员对STIR手抓取20个不同对象的数据集进行了无监督分析，同时执行了一个物体分类任务及一个消融研究以突出软关节传感器的作用。

Result: 实验结果表明，STIR手能够有效区分物体大小、形状和材料硬度，并以高准确率完成物体分类任务。此外，对比传统工业夹具和其他机器人手，STIR手展示了传感器化柔顺关节如何显著提高整体敏感性和保证安全抓握，而无需依赖额外的指尖触觉组件或外部视觉系统。

Conclusion: STIR Hand作为一个轻量级、低成本解决方案，在不牺牲功能性前提下，通过其独特的设计成功地模拟了人类手部的一些关键特性，特别是在抓握多样物品时展现出色的安全性和准确性。这为未来开发更高效、更安全的人机交互界面提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Human hand grasp adaptation depends mainly on the synergy between physical structure and biological feedback. Inspired by this biomechanical principle, the Safe Thumb-Index Robotic (STIR) Hand was developed as a minimal, lightweight, and low-cost two-digit prototype featuring an asymmetric thumb-index configuration. By pairing an underactuated, tendon-driven mechanical design with flexible strain gauges embedded into silicone-encapsulated soft joints, the system achieves passive grasp adaptation while establishing both internal proprioception and external perception. Unsupervised analysis was carried out on a dataset of the STIR hand grasping 20 different objects, along with an object classification task and an ablation study to highlight the contribution of the soft joint sensors. The object classification task discriminated object size, shape, and material stiffness with a high classification accuracy. In contrast to traditional industrial grippers and robotic hands, the STIR Hand demonstrates that sensorized compliant joints significantly improve overall sensitivity and ensure safe grasping, while remaining independent of additional fingertip tactile elements or external vision systems. Finally, a comparison to similar devices grasping identical objects validates the utility of the STIR Hand.

</details>


### [38] [Spectral GS-SLAM: Observability-Aware, Degeneracy-Robust Tracking for Real-Time 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21258)
*Edward Beng Wai Tan,Siew-Kei Lam,Dongshuo Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Spectral GS-SLAM，一种结合ICP和基于特征约束的高效且鲁棒的跟踪框架，通过自适应补偿退化场景中的欠约束方向来缓解数值不稳定性，并引入了高斯感知平面性加权机制以利用3D高斯的内在协方差结构。该方法在无纹理及无结构环境中保持了一致的跟踪性能，同时在非不利条件下也维持了竞争力的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3DGS-SLAM系统虽然能够通过使用传统的特征匹配或基于ICP的跟踪实现实时操作，但在无纹理环境或几何退化的场景中容易失败。为了解决这个问题，提出了一种新的跟踪框架。

Method: 提出的方法名为Spectral GS-SLAM，它整合了ICP与互补的基于特征的约束条件，采用自适应方式补偿退化情况下欠约束的方向，并引入了高斯感知平面性权重机制来表征场景几何并指导信息融合。

Result: 在具有挑战性的TUM RGB-D序列上的广泛评估表明，Spectral GS-SLAM实现了实时性能（40.14 FPS），并在无结构和无特征环境中保持一致的跟踪效果。

Conclusion: Spectral GS-SLAM不仅在退化场景中保持轨迹完整性，而且在常规条件下也表现出色，提供了一种在多种环境下都能有效工作的SLAM解决方案。

Abstract: Recent 3DGS-SLAM systems enable real-time operation by leveraging conventional feature matching or ICP-based tracking, thereby avoiding the heavy dense photometric optimization used in earlier approaches. However, feature matching remains prone to failure in textureless environments, while ICP-based tracking struggles in structureless or geometrically degenerate scenes due to ill-conditioned optimization. To address this issue, we propose Spectral GS-SLAM, an efficient yet robust tracking framework that integrates ICP with complementary feature-based constraints. Our method mitigates numerical instability by adaptively compensating under-constrained directions in degenerate scenarios, without interfering with the shared Gaussian representation used for mapping. We further introduce a Gaussian-aware planarity weighting mechanism that exploits the intrinsic covariance structure of 3D Gaussians to characterize scene geometry and guide information fusion. Extensive evaluations on challenging TUM RGB-D sequences demonstrate that Spectral GS-SLAM achieves real-time performance (40.14 FPS) while maintaining consistent tracking in both structureless and featureless environments. The proposed method preserves trajectory integrity in degenerate scenes while maintaining competitive performance in non-adverse conditions.

</details>


### [39] [RogueRover: Autonomous Rogue Device Localization for Incident Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21264)
*Priyanka Prakash Surve,Asaf Shabtai,Yuval Elovici*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为RogueRover的系统，该系统利用一个四足机器人自主巡逻并收集无线信号强度信息，以在无需预先配置或校准的情况下检测和定位未经授权的无线设备。实验表明，通过多次巡逻，该系统能够将大部分设备的位置误差控制在1米以内，证明了无基础设施的自主定位在实际应用中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理定位未授权无线设备是网络安全操作中的关键瓶颈。尽管可以通过网络侧机制检测这些设备，但确定其物理位置通常需要密集的传感基础设施、特定地点的RF指纹识别或人工检查，这限制了及时响应事件的能力。研究旨在探索是否可以在零配置约束下，使用单一商用机器人自主检测和定位非法无线设备。

Method: 研究人员开发了RogueRover系统，其中包含一个能够自主巡逻的四足机器人。该机器人通过标准802.11接口收集带有空间标签的RSSI测量值，并离线估计设备位置。评估是在真实室内环境中进行的，共进行了11次巡逻运行，部署了6个处于异构传播条件下的非法设备。

Result: 在对62个AP-巡逻会话的评估中，RogueRover单次巡逻定位误差中位数为1.62米，而在多轮聚合后，六分之五的设备被定位在1米范围内。一次盲测验证了整个流程的有效性，从73个观察到的BSSIDs中正确识别出非法设备，并且定位误差分别为0.34米和1.84米。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在零先验条件下，简单的加权质心估计器与参数路径损耗模型相比表现相当甚至更好，这意味着自主巡逻提供的测量覆盖范围是决定定位准确性的主要因素。这项研究表明，无基础设施的自主定位在实践中是可行的，能够在没有额外传感基础设施的情况下促进快速物理事件响应。

Abstract: Physically localizing unauthorized wireless devices remains a critical bottleneck in cyber-physical security operations, where rogue access points can provide entry points for lateral movement and persistent compromise. While such devices can often be detected through network-side mechanisms, determining their physical location typically requires dense sensing infrastructure, site-specific RF fingerprinting, or manual inspection, limiting timely incident response. We investigate whether a single commodity robot can autonomously detect and localize rogue wireless devices under zero-configuration constraints, without RF fingerprinting, pre-installed sensors, or site calibration. We present RogueRover, an end-to-end system in which a quadruped robot autonomously patrols, collects spatially labeled RSSI measurements via a standard 802.11 interface, and estimates device locations offline. We evaluate the system across 11 patrol runs in a real indoor environment, with 6 rogue devices deployed under heterogeneous propagation conditions. Across 62 AP-patrol sessions, RogueRover achieves a median single-patrol localization error of 1.62 m without prior RF knowledge. Under multi-run aggregation, five of six devices are localized within 1 m. A blind trial validates the full pipeline, correctly identifying rogue devices among 73 observed BSSIDs and localizing them with errors of 0.34 m and 1.84 m. Across environments, simple weighted-centroid estimators perform comparably to, or better than, parametric path-loss models, indicating that measurement coverage from autonomous patrols is the primary determinant of localization accuracy under zero-prior constraints. Our results demonstrate that infrastructure-free, autonomous localization is feasible in practice, enabling rapid physical incident response in cyber-physical environments without additional sensing infrastructure.

</details>

*Ahad Jawaid,Yu Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种新的神经动作编解码器（NAC），它借鉴了神经音频编解码器的成功经验，用于压缩机器人动作轨迹，并在多个任务上展示了比现有方法更低的重构误差和更高的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型中使用的离散动作tokenizer在压缩、延迟和下游性能之间存在权衡。受到神经音频编解码器成功的启发，研究者们希望探索一种新的方法来提高动作序列的压缩效率与表现质量。

Method: 提出了一种名为神经动作编解码器(NAC)的新方法，该方法将短时机器人动作轨迹视为多通道一维信号，并采用多尺度RVQGAN架构进行压缩。同时，通过使用简单的时域及非mel谱重建损失替代音频特有的mel频谱目标函数，实现了高保真度的动作自编码。

Result: NAC在LIBERO-10、RoboMimic以及一系列真实世界操作任务上的表现优于分箱、FAST及其他基于VQ的tokenizer，在保证或超越压缩率的同时达到了更低的重构误差和更高的成功率。

Conclusion: 重新利用神经音频编解码器为现代VLA中的学习型动作tokenization提供了一个强大且实用的基础。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models rely on discrete action tokenizers to bridge continuous robot control and autoregressive sequence modeling, yet existing tokenizers often trade off between compression, latency, and downstream performance. We revisit this design through the lens of neural audio codecs-convolutional encoder-decoder architectures with residual vector quantization that serve as the standard front end for audio foundation models. Motivated by their success, we introduce the Neural Action Codec (NAC), which treats short robot action trajectories as multi-channel 1D signals and compresses them using a multi-scale RVQGAN architecture. We observe that audio-specific mel-spectrogram objectives are ill-suited for kinematic signals; however, by replacing them with simple time-domain and non-mel spectral reconstruction losses, audio-codec-style models can autoencode actions with high fidelity without substantial architectural changes. NAC provides a compact, ordered token space via offset codebooks, enabling standard autoregressive policies to operate over short, structured sequences. Meanwhile, a Vocos-style decoder with an ISTFT head and adversarial discriminators recovers smooth, detailed trajectories. Across LIBERO-10, RoboMimic, and a suite of real-world manipulation tasks, NAC achieves lower reconstruction error and higher success rates than binning, FAST, and prior VQ-based tokenizers at comparable or better compression rates. These results demonstrate that repurposed neural audio codecs offer a strong, practical backbone for learned action tokenization in modern VLAs.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮腿机器人结合了轮子在平坦地面上的有效移动性和腿部在崎岖地形中的适应性，但它们在现实环境中的长距离自主导航应用仍较为有限。本研究旨在通过构建适用于这类机器人的控制系统来解决这一问题。

Method: 研究人员扩展了之前为四足机器人设计的仅基于本体感觉的策略，以适应具有16自由度的轮腿机器人Go2-W。同时，针对轮式移动过程中出现的关节过热问题，提出了能够分散负载的新策略。此外，还开发了一套自主导航系统。

Result: 所开发的系统参加了筑波挑战2025活动，在大约2.8公里包含人行道、公园以及楼梯的真实路线上实现了不间断的自主行驶，未因过热而停止。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过深度强化学习方法可以有效地控制轮腿机器人进行长距离自主导航，解决了由于长时间使用导致的关键部位过热的问题。

Abstract: Legged-wheeled robots have long been studied for their potential to combine the efficient flat-ground mobility of wheels with the rough-terrain capability of legs. However, examples of their application to long-range autonomous navigation in real environments remain limited. This paper reports our effort to build a deep reinforcement learning (DRL) based locomotion controller and an autonomous navigation system for the commercially available legged-wheeled robot Go2-W, and to apply them to long-range autonomous navigation in a real environment. For locomotion control, we extended a proprioception-only policy, which we had previously developed for quadruped robots, to the 16-DoF legged-wheeled robot. We also found that wheeled locomotion concentrates the load on the hip joints and causes heat concentration that hinders sustained travel, and obtained a policy that suppresses it by distributing the load. We evaluated the system at the Tsukuba Challenge 2025, demonstrating that it can autonomously traverse an approximately 2.8 km route including sidewalks, a park, and stairs without stopping due to overheating.

</details>


### [42] [Overcoming Imperfect Kinematics in Surgical Robotics Through Sim-to-Real Visuomotor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21396)
*Zhaoxuan Yan,Kaizhong Deng,Zhaoyang Jacopo Hu,George P. Mylonas,Daniel S. Elson*

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于学习策略的方法，通过闭环视觉反馈来主动补偿手术机器人硬件不准确的问题，从而实现实时校正运动学误差，无需依赖完美的物理模型。实验在达芬奇研究平台上验证了该方法的有效性，为更先进的手术自动化铺平了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人的内在运动学不准确性阻碍了其向更高精度和减少外科医生疲劳方向的发展。现有解决办法通常成本高昂且效果有限。

Method: 采用一种学习策略，结合不可靠的内部传感器读数与精确的外部视觉数据，通过闭合视觉反馈来实时纠正运动学错误。该策略基于教师-学生学习框架训练而成。

Result: 所提出的策略被成功应用于达芬奇研究工具包上，并通过实验证明了利用外部视觉克服内部传感器缺陷的基本可行性。

Conclusion: 这项研究提供了一种基础而可靠的控制方法论，有助于推动更加先进稳健的外科手术自动化技术的发展。

Abstract: Robot-Assisted Surgery is integral to modern minimally invasive procedures, with automation emerging as the next frontier to enhance precision and reduce surgeon fatigue. This evolution is largely impeded by the inherent kinematic inaccuracies of surgical robots, where unreliable internal sensors lead to significant control errors. While previous methods attempted to mitigate these issues through complex model-based calibration, they often suffer from high cost and limited effectiveness. This work utilises a learning-policy to actively compensate for hardware inaccuracies using closed-loop visual feedback that was trained from a teacher-student learning framework. The policy can fuse unreliable internal readings with precise external visual data, allowing it to correct for kinematic errors in real time without needing a perfect physical model. The learned policy was successfully deployed on the da Vinci Research Kit, where experiments validated the fundamental feasibility of using external vision to overcome internal sensor deficits. This research provides a foundational and reliable control methodology, paving the way for more advanced and robust surgical automation.

</details>


### [43] [BIT-Nav: Brain-Inspired Trajectory Memory for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21398)
*Rithvik Jonna,Aakash Gurram,Man Namgung,Wyatt Mackey,Tinoosh Mohsenin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为BIT-Nav的框架，它通过引入一个紧凑的学习轨迹记忆来增强现有的视觉-语言模型导航管道，该方法受海马路径整合启发，将空间经验压缩成结构化的片段而非原始感官回放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着导航任务持续时间的增长，选择固定数量的画面变得越来越稀疏，不足以捕捉长距离推理所需的结构化行为信号（如转向模式、累积位移和路径拓扑）。研究者们发现，单纯增加画面数量并不能提高准确性，表明瓶颈在于表示本身而非帧数。

Method: BIT-Nav利用Bi-GRU编码器处理动作与相对姿态序列，并通过一个多正样本InfoNCE对比目标函数训练，旨在学习共享相同行为意图的轨迹前缀。所得嵌入经由轻量级MLP投影到VLM令牌空间中，并作为单个记忆令牌在每个决策步骤时注入，以此条件化模型对任何长度剧集中的结构化运动历史。

Result: 此方法能够以恒定的令牌成本提供关于结构化移动历史的信息，无论情节长度如何，从而改进了现有基于视觉-语言模型的导航系统对于长距离导航的理解能力。

Conclusion: 通过模仿大脑海马区路径整合机制，BIT-Nav为解决长距离导航问题提供了一种新颖而有效的解决方案，强调了对导航过程中结构化行为信号的有效编码的重要性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for embodied navigation rely on selecting a fixed number of frames from a growing trajectory history. As episodes extend, this selection grows increasingly sparse, yet prior work shows no accuracy gain when scaling from 8 to 64 frames, suggesting the bottleneck is not frame quantity but the representation itself. Sparse frame selection cannot capture the structured behavioral signal that long-horizon reasoning requires: turning patterns, cumulative displacement, and path topology.
  We introduce BIT-Nav (Brain-Inspired Trajectory Memory for Navigation), a framework that augments frozen VLM navigation pipelines with a compact learned trajectory memory. Motivated by hippocampal path integration, where spatial experience is compressed into structured episodic traces rather than stored as raw sensory replay, BIT-Nav trains a Bi-GRU encoder over action and relative pose sequences via a multi-positive InfoNCE contrastive objective on trajectory prefixes sharing the same behavioral intent. The resulting embedding is projected into the VLM token space via a lightweight MLP and injected as a single memory token at each decision step, conditioning the model on structured motion history at constant token cost regardless of episode length

</details>


### [44] [Robot Self-Improvement via Human-Video Dynamics Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21406)
*Hanzhi Chen,Anran Zhang,Simon Schaefer,Kejia Chen,Shi Chen,Daniel Cremers,Oier Mees,Stefan Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究展示了如何利用人类视频中的数据来学习与具体实体无关的动作、动态和价值表示，这些表示可以跨机器人实体转移。通过引入一种无需训练的方法——动态引导动作修正（DGAC），该方法能够利用这些模型从失败中恢复，并将每次失败转化为下一次策略更新的监督信息。实验表明，在多个实际操作任务中，这种方法能显著提高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索如何让机器人从人类的学习方式中获取技能，包括观察、实践以及经历失败。虽然之前的研究显示人类视频可以为有用的策略提供初始化，但尚未明确的是，从这些视频中提取的先验知识是否足够支持机器人自身的尝试、评估、纠正和改进。

Method: 提出了一种称为动态引导动作修正(DGAC)的无训练方法，它使用从人类视频中学到的、可跨机器人形态迁移的动作、动态及价值表示来修复失败状态。每当遇到失败时，所学模型会提出并排名纠正措施，从而将失败转变为下次策略更新的指导。

Result: 在七个涉及移动操纵器和静态机械臂的真实世界操控任务上，本方法能够使不同策略架构的成功率从40%提升至81%，证明了基于人类视频先验知识的跨形态机器人自我改进是可行的。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结合人类先验知识与机器人实践中的失败经历，可以促进可扩展的自主策略改进。

Abstract: A central question in robot learning is how to acquire skills from the kinds of data that humans learn from: passive observation, embodied practice, and the experience of failure. Human videos provide the first of these in abundance, and prior work has shown they can initialize useful policies. Far less clear is whether they can support the second and third: whether priors extracted from human videos can ground a robot's own attempts well enough to evaluate them, correct them, and improve from them. In this work, we show that human videos can be used to learn embodiment-agnostic action, dynamics, and value representations that transfer across robot embodiments, providing the predictive foundation required for robots to autonomously improve from their own rollouts and failures. We introduce Dynamics-Guided Action Correction (DGAC), a training-free approach that leverages these adapted models to repair failed states: each failure becomes a query for which the learned models propose and rank corrective actions, turning failures into supervision for the next policy update. Across seven real-world manipulation tasks spanning both a mobile manipulator and a static manipulator arm, our approach improves success rates from 40% to 81% across multiple policy backbones, demonstrating cross-embodiment robot self-improvement from human-video priors. These results show that human priors and robot failures can be combined to enable scalable autonomous policy improvement. Project page: https://ethz-mrl.github.io/robot-self-improvement-website/.

</details>


### [45] [Temporal logics and formal synthesis for robot planning and control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21438)
*Jana Tumova,Joris Verhagen,Matti Vahs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了如何使用时态逻辑来规范机器人的行为，并讨论了从离散图和基于游戏的方法到基于采样的运动规划、轨迹优化以及基于控制证书的综合等多种形式化综合原则。此外，还概述了在现实世界机器人技术中部署形式化综合所面临的挑战，强调了建模保真度、计算可处理性以及可以实现的各种严格保证之间的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人从受控环境转移到现实世界环境中，确保它们按预期执行变得越来越重要。为了达到这个目标，关键步骤之一是严谨地定义所需的机器人行为，捕捉复杂的时间、空间和逻辑要求。

Method: 本文采用了线性和信号时态逻辑作为表达机器人随时间行为的规范语言，并讨论了从离散图-和基于游戏的方法到基于采样的运动规划、轨迹优化以及基于控制证明的形式合成原理。

Result: 通过使用时态逻辑进行行为规范，结合不同的形式化综合方法，能够为机器人提供满足特定需求的行为策略，并且具有可证明的保障。同时指出了实际应用中的挑战，包括模型准确性、计算可行性和可以获得的保障类型之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 文章总结了时态逻辑作为一种强大的工具，在指定复杂的机器人行为方面的作用；同时强调了形式化综合方法对于实现这些行为规范的重要性。面对实际应用中的挑战，需要在模型保真度、计算效率与可获得的保障之间找到平衡点。

Abstract: As robots move from controlled environments into real-world settings, it becomes increasingly crucial to ensure that they perform as expected. A key step toward that goal is a rigorous specification of the desired robot behavior, capturing intricate temporal, spatial, and logical requirements. Complementing this, plan and control synthesis methods are needed to fulfill these specifications with provable guarantees. This manuscript presents temporal logics - particularly linear and signal temporal logic - as expressive specification languages for robot behavior over time. We then discuss principles of formal synthesis, from discrete graph- and game-based approaches to sampling-based motion planning, trajectory optimization, and control-certificate-based synthesis. Finally, we outline challenges in deploying formal synthesis in real-world robotics, emphasizing the interplay between modeling fidelity, computational tractability, and the types of rigorous guarantees that can be achieved.

</details>


### [46] [Manipulider: A Multi-Engine Buoyancy-Controlled Robot for Thrusterless Underwater Gliding and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21461)
*Yitao Jiang,Yewei Huang,Weizhi Cao,Mingi Jeong,Alberto Quattrini Li,Luyang Zhao,Muhao Chen,Devin Balkcom*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 介绍了一种名为Manipulider的新型水下机器人，它利用浮力引擎实现无推进器滑翔式移动和基于姿态的操作，并通过磁性模块化接口快速更换有效载荷。研究展示了其设计、校准过程及控制架构，并通过实验验证了其在倾斜角度控制、可控浮力位移范围以及无推进器的有效载荷运输概念验证等方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种能够进行无推进器滑行运动并支持基于姿态调整来执行操作任务的水下机器人系统，同时提供一个快速交换传感器或抓取工具等负载的磁性模块化接口。旨在解决传统水下机器人面临的推进器缠绕风险等问题。

Method: 通过围绕机体分布的四个基于注射器的浮力引擎共同调节净浮力和浮力中心位置，使得机器人能够在没有持续推力的情况下保持大角度倾斜并通过静态力平衡避免推进器缠绕的风险。此外还介绍了机器人的机械与电气设计、校准程序及其控制系统架构。

Result: 实验结果表明，在不携带外部负载情况下安装有抓手的Manipulider可以达到每个引擎40毫升（总共约160克）的可控浮力位移范围；单引擎最大静态稳定倾斜角度为64.6度，双引擎配置下则为61.8度；并且成功演示了倾斜角调控、受控上升/下降基本动作以及无推进器的概念验证性抓手搬运序列。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的Manipulider水下机器人展示出良好的性能，在不需要使用推进器的前提下实现了有效的姿态控制与操作功能，证明了该设计方案的有效性和潜在应用价值。

Abstract: The Manipulider is a buoyancy-actuated underwater robot that enables thrusterless, glide-like locomotion and attitude-based manipulation, while providing a magnetic modular interface for rapid payload swapping (e.g., a gripper or sensors). Four syringe-based buoyancy engines distributed around the body jointly regulate net buoyancy and the center of buoyancy, allowing the vehicle to maintain large tilt angles through static force balance without continuous thrust and to avoid propeller entanglement risks. We present the mechanical and electrical design, calibration procedure, and control architecture. Experiments with a gripper attached (no external payload) show a controllable buoyancy-displacement range of 40 mL per engine ({\approx}160 g total buoyancy authority), maximum statically stable tilts of 64.6° (single-engine) and 61.8° (dual-engine), and representative vertical and tilt-transition dynamics. We further demonstrate tilt regulation, controlled ascent/descent primitives, and a proof-of-concept gripper-based payload-transport sequence without thrusters.

</details>


### [47] [ASCII Art Turns LLMs into VLA Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21470)
*Yitao Jiang,Roy Xing,Luyang Zhao,Brian Plancher,Muhao Chen,Devin Balkcom*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究展示了仅使用文本的大型语言模型（LLM）如何通过将视觉观察转换为ASCII表示来适应成为类似于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）控制器。这种基于ASCII的视觉接口允许现有的LLM训练和部署架构有效利用视觉状态、遵循自然语言指令并生成可执行的动作。在2D操作基准测试中，无论是模拟还是实际操作器上，所得到的控制器都能够识别任务相关的实体并规划可行的动作序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）扩展到包含行动监督以构建VLA控制器时，通常需要大量的数据和计算资源。本研究旨在探索一种更轻量级的方法，即通过将视觉信息转化为ASCII文本格式，使纯文本的大规模语言模型能够处理视觉输入，并据此执行相应的动作。

Method: 研究者们采用了文本转ASCII图像表示的方式，让原本设计用于处理纯文本输入的大型语言模型能够理解和响应包含视觉信息的任务。他们对多个不同家族和规模的语言模型进行了微调与对比实验，同时运用了基于规划的教师提供的专家演示以及DAgger方法来进行迭代改进。

Result: 实验结果表明，在二维操作任务中，无论是虚拟环境还是真实世界中的物理机械臂控制，经过调整后的模型都能够成功识别关键对象并按指示完成一系列连贯的操作步骤。

Conclusion: 这项工作提出了一种新的方法，利用ASCII渲染作为从图像到文本的轻量级且易于解释的模态桥梁，从而使得纯文本基础的语言模型也能够胜任一些视觉-语言-动作任务。这种方法不仅简化了传统VLA流程，还为未来的研究开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: Vision--Language--Action (VLA) controllers are often built by extending vision--language models (VLMs) with action supervision, relying on multimodal backbones with large data and compute requirements. We demonstrate that a text-only large language model (LLM) can be adapted into a VLA-style controller when visual observations are rendered into a text input using an ASCII representation. This ASCII-as-vision interface enables existing training and deployment stacks for LLMs to efficiently condition on visual state, follow natural-language instructions, and produce constrained, executable actions. We fine-tune and compare multiple LLMs and VLMs across model families and scales, using both expert demonstrations from a planning-based teacher, as well as DAgger for iterative improvement. In a 2D manipulation benchmark, in both simulation and on a physical manipulator, the resulting controllers can identify task-relevant entities and plan feasible action sequences. Our results suggest that ASCII rendering can serve as a lightweight, interpretable modality bridge from images to text, complementing conventional VLA pipelines, and opening directions for VLA research with text-only backbones.

</details>


### [48] [Decoupling the Declarative from the Procedural in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21496)
*Nikolaos Tsagkas,Andreas Sochopoulos,Chris Xiaoxuan Lu,Oisin Mac Aodha,Alexandros Kouris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的VLA模型w$^{2}$VLA，通过重组信息流以解耦声明性知识和程序性知识，从而实现对新物体前所未有的零样本技能迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型在分布内操作表现优秀，但对空间、语义及任务上的微小变化适应性差。主要原因在于这些模型无法将声明性知识（即概念和实体语义）与程序性知识（即如何做某事）进行有效分离，这限制了它们向新对象进行零样本技能转移的能力。

Method: 提出了w$^{2}$VLA模型，它通过一种组合且可解释的方式利用视觉、空间以及技能信息来调节机器人状态序列，而不是简单地将所有多模态标记从VLM编码器传递给一个大型不透明的动作专家变压器。

Result: 实验表明，这种模块化方法成功地分离了知识表示，使得行为克隆更加稳健，并且对于不同且未见过的对象展现了前所未有的零样本技能转移能力。

Conclusion: w$^{2}$VLA模型通过改进信息流结构解决了现有VLA模型中存在的根本瓶颈问题，为通用机器人代理提供了更加强大和灵活的操作能力。

Abstract: Deploying generalist robotic agents in the real world requires transferable skills. Specifically, a policy trained to clone a behavior from object-specific demonstrations must generalize beyond that object, otherwise data collection requirements become intractable. Recently, fine-tuning of pre-trained billion-parameter Vision-Language Models (VLMs), initially on large-scale robot datasets and then on fewer scenario-specific demonstrations, has emerged as the predominant paradigm for designing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. While these policies achieve state-of-the-art manipulation performance in-distribution, they remain brittle to minor spatial, semantic, and task variations. In this work, we address the inability of current models to decouple the declarative (i.e., concepts and entity semantics) from the procedural knowledge (i.e., how to do something) encoded in their parameters, which is a fundamental bottleneck for zero-shot skill transfer to novel objects. To address this, we propose w$^{2}$VLA, a new VLA model with restructured information flow. Rather than feeding all multimodal tokens from the VLM encoder into a large, opaque transformer-based action expert, our approach modulates the robot state sequence with visual, spatial, and skill information in a compositional and interpretable manner. Unlike popular, state-of-the-art VLAs, we show that our modular approach successfully decouples knowledge representations, enabling robust behavior cloning and unprecedented zero-shot skill transfer capabilities across dissimilar, unseen objects.

</details>


### [49] [UniviewVLA: A Unified Multiview Vision-Language-Action Model with World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21501)
*Tao Xu,Runhao Zhang,Zhijian Huang,Jiayi Guan,Jiaxin Wang,Yifan Ding,Yong-Lu Li,Long Chen,Guang Chen,Jinghui Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为UniviewVLA的多视图视觉-语言-动作模型，通过仅使用标准双摄像头观察来预测动作，并能揭示遮挡线索和建模未来场景演变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人操作中的遮挡任务仍然是一个瓶颈。现有解决方案要么需要额外的物理相机，要求训练-推理相机一致性；要么依赖于显式的3D重建，但计算成本高。此外，这两种方法都依赖于标准的代理视角和手腕视角观测，未能捕捉到遮挡信息及未来场景的变化。

Method: 开发了UniviewVLA，这是一种结合了世界建模功能的统一多视图视觉-语言-动作模型，能够从标准的两相机观察中推断出多视图场景演化来进行动作预测。还引入了Motion-Informative Token Compression技术以加速推理同时保持预测准确性，并提出了无需训练的动作熵视图选择方法，可根据不同推理阶段动态识别最具有动作信息量的视图。

Result: 在LIBERO和CALVIN ABCD to D等无遮挡基准测试上分别达到了95.8%和4.60的成绩。对于定制的聚焦于遮挡的任务，它将成功率从40.0%提高到了73.3%，平均实际机器人成功率提高了33.4个百分点。

Conclusion: UniviewVLA展示了在不牺牲标准无遮挡基准性能的情况下，显著增强了对遮挡任务的关注能力。

Abstract: Occluded tasks remain a bottleneck in robot manipulation. Existing solutions either deploy additional physical cameras requiring training-inference camera parity, or rely on explicit 3D reconstruction with high computational cost. Moreover, both approaches rely on standard agent-view and wrist-view observations, while failing to capture occlusion information and future scene evolution. To this end, we propose UniviewVLA, a unified multiview Vision-Language-Action model with world modeling, which infers multiview scene evolution for action prediction from only standard two-camera observations. We demonstrate that by leveraging generated multiview future views from the world model, UniviewVLA reveals occluded cues and models future scene evolution, improving action prediction and removing the need for extra hardware or explicit reconstruction. Besides, to accelerate inference while preserving prediction accuracy, UniviewVLA develops Motion-Informative Token Compression, which compresses each generated view from 625 to 16 tokens and reduces per-view latency from 6-7s to 0.2-0.3s. UniviewVLA also proposes training-free Action-Entropy View Selection, which dynamically identifies the most action-informative view at different inference stages. Extensive experiments show that UniviewVLA achieves 95.8% on LIBERO and 4.60 on CALVIN ABCD to D, both standard occlusion-free benchmarks. On customized occlusion-focused tasks, it improves success rate from 40.0% to 73.3%, and average real-robot success rate by 33.4 points, demonstrating stronger occlusion-focused performance without sacrificing standard occlusion-free benchmarks.

</details>


### [50] [A Stitch in Time Saves Nine: Preserving Policy Compatibility Under Perception Updates in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21509)
*Yueyuan Li,Yifei Xiao,Mingyang Jiang,Xiang Zuo,Songan Zhang,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的潜空间对齐方法来解决端到端自动驾驶系统中的模型缝合问题，实验表明该方法能够有效保持更新后的感知模块与下游策略模块之间的兼容性，并显著减少适应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对端到端自动驾驶系统中感知模型更新导致表示变化进而影响固定下游策略性能的问题，提出了通过轻量级潜空间对齐来维持策略兼容性的假设。

Method: 研究了包括线性和卷积缝合器在内的低复杂度模型缝合方法，用于恢复更新后的感知模块与冻结的下游策略模块之间的兼容性。

Result: 实验显示，在多种感知更新情况下（如随机初始化、传感器配置及训练域的变化），缝合技术能有效保留下游驾驶行为；在最具挑战性的跨域场景下，卷积缝合技术保持了超过91%的无偏移驾驶评分，并将适应时间从22.18小时缩短至0.91小时。

Conclusion: 模型缝合提供了一种有效且计算效率高的解决方案，以维护端到端自动驾驶系统的性能，避免了重新训练或微调带来的高昂成本。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving systems tightly couple perception and decision-making through latent representations. Consequently, updates to perception models can alter these representations and degrade the performance of downstream policies that remain fixed. Existing solutions typically rely on policy retraining or architectural decoupling, both of which incur substantial computation and validation costs. In this paper, we formulate the model stitching problem for end-to-end autonomous driving and test the hypothesis that policy compatibility can be preserved through lightweight latent-space alignment. We study low-complexity model stitching methods, including linear and convolutional stitchers, for restoring compatibility between updated perception modules and frozen downstream policy modules. Experiments demonstrate that stitching effectively preserves downstream driving behavior under diverse perception updates, including changes in random initialization, sensor configuration, and training domain. In the most challenging cross-domain setting from nuScenes to CARLA, convolutional stitching retains over 91\% of the no-shift driving score while reducing adaptation time from \SI{22.18}{h} to \SI{0.91}{h}. These results suggest that model stitching provides an effective and computationally efficient alternative to retraining or fine-tuning for maintaining end-to-end autonomous driving systems. The model will be open-sourced upon paper acceptance at https://github.com/SCP-CN-001/model-stitching to support further research and development in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [51] [LOGOS: LiDAR-Only Gaussian Elevation Splatting for Unified Tiny Obstacle Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21527)
*Nan Ming,Yeqiang Qian,Chunxiang Wang,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为LOGOS的仅基于LiDAR的小型障碍物分割系统，该系统通过将路面建模为二维高斯基元的连续混合体，并通过高精度高度估计来区分小型障碍物。实验表明，在城市和非道路场景中，LOGOS在处理退化点云区域和具有挑战性的非道路场景时表现优于其他最先进方法，同时保持实时效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LiDAR的方法在处理像路缘石、碎石及坑洞这样的小型障碍物时表现不佳，特别是在非道路环境中遇到LiDAR扫描质量下降的情况下。因此，需要一种新的方法来提高对于这些小障碍物的识别准确性。

Method: LOGOS系统将地面视为一系列二维高斯基础元素的组合，并且通过精确的高度测量来辨别小型障碍物。它不依赖于RGB训练迭代过程，而是直接通过考虑自由空间意识的初始化来估算高斯参数，并利用平滑性约束逐步去除非道路成分。然后，通过一个新颖的法线感知高度喷射函数计算逐点带符号距离，以确保对平坦及斜坡地形的鲁棒性。

Result: LOGOS在从城市移动环境到采矿运输非道路环境等多种异质点云帧基准测试中进行了评估，数据来自不同的LiDAR传感器，显示了在点密度、地形粗糙度以及障碍类型上的大范围变化。实验结果证明，无论是在道路还是非道路场景下，尤其是在点云质量较差和极具挑战性的非道路条件下，LOGOS都显著优于现有的最新技术，同时还能保持实时性能。

Conclusion: LOGOS作为一种创新的仅基于LiDAR的小型障碍物检测方案，成功地解决了现有方法在应对小型障碍物特别是复杂非道路环境下所面临的难题。其独特的设计使其不仅能够高效准确地识别小型障碍物，而且能够在各种恶劣条件下保持稳定的工作状态。

Abstract: Robust obstacle segmentation is essential for the safety of intelligent robots, where LiDAR-based perception systems play a fundamental role in the robot-environment interaction. While extensive LiDAR-based approaches have demonstrated high performance on common obstacles in urban scenarios, their results on tiny obstacles such as curbs, gravel, and potholes remain unsatisfactory due to the significant similarity between tiny obstacles and inherent road undulations. Moreover, their segmentation accuracy even deteriorates sharply when the LiDAR scans suffer from degradation in challenging off-road scenes. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose LOGOS, a LiDAR-only unified tiny obstacle segmentation system, which models the road surface as a continuous mixture of 2D Gaussian primitives and distinguishes tiny obstacles via high-presicion elevation estimation. Unlike existing Gaussian splatting methods that rely on iterative RGB training, LOGOS is a backpropagation-free LiDAR-only approach. It directly estimates Gaussian parameters via a freespace-aware initialization by incrementally pruning non-road primitives using smoothness constraints. Subsequently, pointwise signed distances are computed via a novel normal-aware elevation splatting function, ensuring robustness to both flat and sloped terrains. We evaluate LOGOS on a highly heterogeneous benchmark of point cloud frames collected from urban mobility scenarios and mining haulage off-road environments. These data are practically acquired using different LiDAR sensors and exhibit large variations in point density, terrain roughness, and obstacle types. Experiments on the road and off-road scenes demonstrate that LOGOS significantly outperforms other state-of-the-art methods, particularly in degraded point cloud regions and challenging off-road scenarios, while maintaining real-time efficiency.

</details>


### [52] [Robot Critics that Sweat the Small Stuff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21572)
*Sruthi Sudhakar,Junbang Liang,Sreehari Rammohan,Pavel Tokmakov,Richard Zemel,Carl Vondrick*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过构建成功和失败轨迹的成对进展监督来微调批评者的方法，该方法在细粒度进展推理和微妙失败检测方面表现出色，并且能够识别成功的候选动作以执行，从而提高策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大型视觉-语言模型在推理过程中作为评判者指导机器人策略很有用，但闭环机器人操作需要判断成功与失败之间细微的视觉差异，这对当前的VLMs来说仍然是个挑战。

Method: 通过使用从策略中获得的成功和失败轨迹来构造成对进展监督，以此方法来微调评判者。此外，利用一个动作条件视频模型预测来自策略的几个候选动作的视觉效果。

Result: 微调后的评判者在细粒度进展推理和微妙失败检测上表现优异，超过了先前的进展推理基线。同时，实验证明该评判者能正确识别出要执行的成功候选动作，分别将现实世界任务和模拟任务中的平均策略成功率提高了11%和5.9%。

Conclusion: 所提出的微调方法有效提升了机器人在闭环操作过程中的表现，特别是在区分细微视觉差异以及选择有利行动方面。

Abstract: Large vision-language models contain several priors about the world and object interactions, making them useful critics during inference to steer robot policies towards success. However, closed-loop robot manipulation requires judging small visual differences between success and failure, which remains a challenge for current VLMs. We introduce a method to fine-tune critics by constructing pairwise progress supervision using success and failure rollouts obtained from a policy. Our fine-tuned critic excels at fine-grained progress reasoning and subtle failure detection, outperforming prior progress reasoning baselines. Additionally, we use an action-conditioned video model to predict the visual effect of several candidate actions sampled from a policy, and show that our critic can correctly identify successful candidates to execute, improving the average policy success rate by 11% across real-world tasks and 5.9% across simulation tasks.

</details>


### [53] [Conflict-Aware Switching for CBF-CLF-Based Multi-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21577)
*Rohan Walia,Kevin Leahy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种冲突感知切换策略，用于在CBF-CLF-QP控制下的多目标到达-避免场景中减少约束冲突，从而降低完成时间和超时率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的使用CBF和CLF的二次规划方法在安全控制方面存在性能下降的问题，特别是在多目标情况下，现有解决方法要么计算成本高，要么过于保守。

Method: 开发一种能够检测高冲突条件并在可用的标称控制目标之间进行切换的策略，以减少这些冲突。该方法应用于基于CBF-CLF-QP控制的多智能体、多目标到达-避免情境。

Result: 与顺序遍历目标的基本策略相比，所提出的方法减少了任务完成时间和超时发生率。

Conclusion: 通过采用冲突感知切换策略，在保持安全约束的同时，提高了所有标称控制目标的实现性能。

Abstract: Quadratic programs (QPs) using Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) and Control Lyapunov Functions (CLFs) are widely used for safe control in reach-and-avoid navigation. However, the inherently conflicting nature of CBF and CLF constraints can lead to performance degradation, including slowdowns and deadlocks. This issue is exacerbated in multi-goal scenarios, where multiple nominal control objectives must be satisfied under shared safety constraints. Existing approaches for preemptive safety are often computationally expensive or overly conservative, while methods that relax or switch between nominal objectives are not well-suited for sequential goal-to-goal navigation. To address these limitations, we propose a conflict-aware switching strategy that detects high-conflict conditions and switches between available nominal control objectives to reduce constraint conflict. We apply this approach to multi-agent, multi-goal reach-and-avoid scenarios under CBF-CLF-QP control. Compared to a baseline sequential goal traversal strategy, our method reduces both completion time and timeout rates, demonstrating improved performance in satisfying all nominal control objectives while respecting safety constraints.

</details>


### [54] [VQActFlow: Vector-Quantized Action Mode Steering for Multi-Task Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21600)
*Zhigen Zhao,Mark Leggiero,Yipu Chen,Haoran Liu,Yifan Wu,Huishu Xue,Sirui Zhan,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为VQActFlow的多任务操作策略，通过将连续动作分块并转换为离散代码序列，以提高机器人基于演示学习不同任务时的表现。该方法在多种平台上均优于现有的连续和离然基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从演示中学习多任务机器人操作策略面临挑战，传统方法需要单一网络根据语言和视觉环境从多模态演示分布中选择不同的行动模式。错误的选择可能导致执行不正确的任务或场景中不可行的动作。

Method: 提出了VQActFlow，一种能够将动作片段标记化并生成代码序列的多任务操作策略。它使用变分流匹配技术，并在整个生成过程中保持对动作模式的明确偏好。此外，通过无分类器引导的语言条件指导以及一个提供补充可行性信号的学习代码本批评家来指导这种偏好，从而引导模式承诺。

Result: VQActFlow在LIBERO仿真基准、Unitree G1类人机器人全身拾取放置任务以及ALOHA风格双手机器人接触丰富任务等三个平台上进行了评估。结果表明，在这些基准测试中，VQActFlow的表现优于连续和离散基线方法。

Conclusion: VQActFlow通过将连续动作转化为离散代码序列的方式，有效地解决了多任务机器人操作中面临的挑战，显示出在多样化平台上的优越性能。

Abstract: Multi-task robot manipulation policies are challenging to learn from demonstration because traditionally a single network must select among qualitatively different action modes from a multimodal demonstration distribution, conditioned on language and visual context. A wrong mode selection means executing the wrong task or an action infeasible in the scene. Tokenizing continuous actions into a learned discrete codebook separates these modes at the representation level, offering structural advantages for multi-task learning. We propose VQActFlow, a multi-task manipulation policy that tokenizes action chunks and generates code sequences via Variational Flow Matching. VQActFlow maintains an explicit preference over action modes throughout generation. Inference-time guidance acts on this preference to steer mode commitment. We instantiate this with classifier-free guidance over language conditioning, which steers the policy toward the instructed action mode, and a learned codebook critic that supplies a complementary feasibility signal. We evaluate VQActFlow on three platforms: the LIBERO simulation benchmarks, a Unitree G1 humanoid performing whole-body pick-and-place, and an ALOHA-style bimanual platform performing contact-rich tasks. Across these benchmarks, VQActFlow outperforms both continuous and discrete baselines.

</details>


### [55] [Energy-based Compositional Diffusion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21646)
*Tao Sun,Utkarsh Aashu Mishra,Jiaxin Lu,Danfei Xu,Iro Armeni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于能量的组合扩散器(ECD)，通过将全局轨迹定义为局部桥接势能之和的最小化来解决长时域机器人任务的问题，该方法在保证接近启发式拼接方法推理速度的同时，在一系列OGBench拼接任务上达到了最先进的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的组合扩散规划者在解决长时间范围内的机器人任务时，往往依赖于局部预测的启发式拼接。然而，这种拼接更新通常构成一个非保守场，并不数学上对应于任何有效的全局轨迹对数密度函数。

Method: 提出了Energy-based Compositional Diffuser (ECD)框架，该框架通过最小化局部桥接势能之和来公式化全局轨迹。此外，还引入了一种基于马尔可夫的分数近似法，以单个块三对角线求解的方式计算反应项，保持了与计划视野线性的时间复杂度。

Result: 实验表明，ECD在一系列OGBench拼接任务中取得了最先进水平的成功率，同时其推理速度几乎可以与启发式拼接方法相媲美。

Conclusion: ECD提供了一个新的视角来处理长时域机器人任务，通过采用能量基础的方法确保生成的轨迹更加符合物理定律，并且在效率上也表现出色。

Abstract: Compositional diffusion planners aim to solve long-horizon robotic tasks using short training trajectories. Yet, current approaches often rely on the heuristic stitching of local predictions. We show that the resulting stitched update is generally a non-conservative field} that does not mathematically correspond to any valid global trajectory log-density function. We propose Energy-based Compositional Diffuser (ECD), a framework that formulates the global trajectory as the minimizer of the sum of local bridge potentials. This energy-based perspective defines a conservative correction field and contains a boundary reaction term that heuristic stitching omits. To enable efficient inference, we further introduce a Markov-based score approximation that computes the reaction term via a single block-tridiagonal solve, maintaining time complexity linear in the planning horizon. Empirically, ECD achieves state-of-the-art success rates on a range of OGBench stitching tasks, while nearly matching the inference speed of heuristic stitching methods. Code is available at https://github.com/GradientSpaces/ECD.

</details>


### [56] [Online Learning of Robust Legged Odometry with Minimal Exteroceptive Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21669)
*Abhijeet M. Kulkarni,Yuze Du,Guoquan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种即插即用的稳健腿部里程计系统，通过利用外部感知运动管道作为连续监督信号来训练从本体感觉数据直接学习的速度神经网络，并使用不变EKF（InEKF）融合学习到的本体感觉或外部感知速度以及IMU数据。该方法在外部感知失效时能无缝切换到使用学习到的本体感觉模型，从而实现对新硬件适应性强且鲁棒性高的腿部机器人运动估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多传感器融合用于腿部机器人的状态估计需要仔细的传感器校准和特定于平台的运动学建模，这使得部署变得复杂。工业包装的外部感知传感器可以提供准确的动作追踪，但在感知退化的条件下仍显脆弱。因此，研究者开发了这种无需明确外部-内部感知校准或系统运动学建模的即插即用、鲁棒性的腿部里程计系统。

Method: 研究者采用了一种方法，该方法利用已建立的外部感知运动流程作为连续监控信号，以训练基于本体感受数据直接在线学习的速度神经网络。然后使用不变扩展卡尔曼滤波器(InEKF)来融合学到的本体或外部感知速度（如果有的话）与IMU数据。当由于环境退化导致外部感知失败时，系统能够无缝地回退到使用学到的本体模型。

Result: 研究展示了该方法跨不同四足平台的应用，证明了其平台无关性和易于部署的特点，在应对具有挑战性的场景时保持稳健的运动估计方面取得了令人鼓舞的结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的腿式机器人里程计算法能够自适应地处理新硬件并维持稳定的运动估算性能，即使是在感知条件下降的情况下也能依靠学习到的本体感觉模型继续工作，表明了其在实际应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Robust locomotion and navigation for legged robots relies heavily on dependable odometry. Traditional multi-sensor fusion for such state estimation requires meticulous sensor calibration and platform-specific kinematic modeling, which complicates deployment. Industrially packaged exteroceptive sensors can provide accurate motion tracking but remain vulnerable to perceptually degraded conditions. We thus develop a plug-and-play, robust legged odometry system that eliminates the need for explicit exteroceptive-to-proprioceptive calibration or system kinematic modeling. Our approach leverages established exteroceptive motion pipelines as a continuous supervisory signal to train an online learned velocity neural network directly from proprioceptive data. An Invariant EKF (InEKF) is then used to fuse the learned proprioceptive or exteroceptive velocity (if any) and IMU data. When exteroception fails due to environmental degradation, the system seamlessly falls back to using the learned proprioceptive model, yielding a resilient legged odometry that readily adapts to new hardware. We demonstrate the platform-agnostic, easily deployable nature of our approach on different quadruped platforms, showcasing promising results in maintaining robust motion estimation across challenging scenarios.

</details>


### [57] [Imitation from Heterogeneous Demonstrations using Grounded Latent-Action World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21672)
*Tianyou Wang,Anson Lei,Joe Watson,Ingmar Posner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预测的行动表示方法，通过使用一个共享潜在行动空间的生成模型(GLAM)来学习异构数据源中的行动转移。实验表明，在模拟和现实世界的五个操作任务中，GLAM对齐策略显著优于行为克隆基线和其他潜在行动方法，平均提高了48%的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习已成为学习视觉运动策略的强大范例，但其泛化性和稳定性受到所需演示数据规模和质量的限制。为了解决这一问题，作者提出利用更丰富但异质的数据源，这些数据源在动作空间上存在差异，并且经常缺乏动作标签。与依赖于启发式和手工设计的对齐技术的现有协同训练方法不同，作者认为行动表示应该基于预测：即对环境产生相同效果的动作，无论其来源如何，都应共享相同的表示。

Method: 提出了名为GLAM（Grounded Latent-Action World Model）的方法，它是一对具有跨数据源共享潜在行动空间的生成模型，该空间通过跨源一致地预测未来观察结果而得到巩固。这个潜在行动空间被用来训练下游的行为克隆(BC)策略，这些策略将观察映射到潜在行动，并将它们解码回机器人行动，从而提供了一种从异构数据中学习的范例。

Result: 实验证明，GLAM能够成功学习一个有助于跨带或不带动作标签的数据源进行动作转移的对齐潜在动作空间。在模拟和真实世界中的五个操纵任务中，与行为克隆基线及先前的潜在动作方法相比，GLAM对齐策略显著提高了任务成功率，平均改善了48%。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了一种新的模仿学习框架——GLAM，它通过构建一个基于预测效果的统一潜在行动表示，有效提升了从异构数据源中学习的能力。这种方法不仅克服了传统模仿学习对于高质量演示数据的高度依赖，而且在多种任务环境中展示了优越的表现。

Abstract: Imitation learning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for learning visuomotor policies, but its generalisation and stability are limited by the scale and quality of demonstration data needed. A promising direction is to leverage more abundant but heterogeneous data sources, which differ in action space and often lack action labels altogether. Existing co-training approaches that combine heterogeneous data sources rely on heuristic and hand-engineered alignment techniques. In contrast, we argue that action representations should be grounded in prediction: actions that produce the same effect on the environment should share the same representation, regardless of their sources. To this end, we instantiate this principle by using a grounded latent-action world model (GLAM), a pair of generative models with a shared latent action space across data sources that is grounded by predicting future observations consistently across sources. This latent action space is used to train downstream behavioural cloning (BC) policies which map observations to latent actions and decode them back to robot actions, providing a paradigm for learning from heterogeneous data. Empirically, we demonstrate that GLAM successfully learns an aligned latent action space that facilitates action transfer across data sources with and without action labels. Across five manipulation tasks in simulation and in the real world, GLAM-aligned policies significantly outperform BC baselines and prior latent-action methods, achieving an average of +48% improvement in task success rate with the same data-scarce setting. Videos and code are available at https://viccccciv.github.io/glam/.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在微流控平台中，由于通道边界和层流条件的动态控制有限，通常会限制流体传输和相互作用。因此，开发一种能够远程控制复杂变形的软材料成为必要，以改善流体操控及增强微混合效果。

Method: 采用基于模板的方法对软膜进行磁性编程，使其能够在外部磁场的影响下可控地变形为正弦波形。将该膜集成到微通道中，可以将被动的通道壁转变为动态可变的界面。

Conclusion: 这项工作提出了一种用于下一代微流控和芯片实验室系统的无线控制变形界面的多功能平台，展示了在微流控应用中利用形状可编程磁性软膜作为主动组件的可能性。

Abstract: The capability to encode spatially distinct magnetization patterns within soft materials enables remote control over complex deformations. This characteristic is especially important for microfluidic platforms, where limited dynamic control of channel boundaries and laminar flow conditions usually restrict fluid transport and interactions. The present study introduces a shape-programmable magnetic soft membrane actuator as an active microchannel component that can dynamically modulate its shape under magnetic fields and therefore the microfluidic environment. The membrane is magnetically programmed using a template-based approach, which allows it to be controllably deformed in the form of a sinusoid under the influence of an external magnetic field. The membrane's integration into a microchannel converts a passive channel wall into a dynamically changeable interface, allowing active fluid manipulation and enhancing micromixing in laminar flow conditions. The proposed approach establishes a versatile platform for wirelessly controlled deformable interfaces in next-generation microfluidic and lab-on-chip systems.

</details>

Main category: cs.RO

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管磁性软体机器人在小规模软体机器人中实现了复杂的形状变形和运动动力学，并且在人体胃肠道的目标药物递送方面展现出了巨大潜力，但要实现对液体载荷向所需组织界面的受控定向释放，同时保留已编码的形状变形和移动能力仍然存在挑战。

Method: 采用特定空间分布的磁化轮廓设计了磁性软体机器人，使释放接口能够与外部磁场的方向对齐。此外，在机器人内部嵌入了互连微孔弹性体用于储存水性载荷，并使用一层薄薄的微晶蜡密封释放口以隔离运输过程中存储的液体载荷。通过增强外部磁场强度机械破裂蜡封层来触发释放。

Result: 该方法允许通过外部磁场的方向和强度解耦控制定向翻转、移动和触发释放。实验表明，这种方法可以有效地将定向目标液体载荷释放、形状变形和移动结合起来。

Conclusion: 报告的控制定向释放策略整合了定向目标液体载荷释放、形状变形以及移动功能，为胃肠道应用中的目标药物递送提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Magnetically programmable soft elastomers enable complex shape morphing and locomotion dynamics in small scale soft robots under external magnetic fields. Benefiting from their programmed deformation and wireless actuation capabilities, magnetic soft robots have emerged as promising platforms for targeted drug delivery, especially in human gastrointestinal tract. However, achieving controlled directional liquid cargo release toward desired tissue interface while preserving the encoded shape morphing and locomotion capabilities remain a significant challenge. Here, we report a new design strategy that employs an optimized magnetization profile to enable controlled directional release of aqueous cargo without compromising shape morphing and locomotion capabilities. Magnetic soft robots with a specific spatially distributed magnetization profile allow directional alignment of the release interface with the orientation of the external magnetic field. This orientation control ensures active alignment of the release interface toward the intestinal wall prior to drug release. An interconnected microporous elastomer is embedded within the robot for aqueous cargo storage, while a thin microcrystalline wax layer seals the release opening hole to isolate the stored liquid cargo from external environment during transport. Triggered release is achieved by mechanically rupturing the wax sealing layer under a higher magnitude external magnetic field. Controlled directional flipping, locomotion, and triggered release are decoupled through external magnetic field's direction and strength. The controlled directional release strategy reported here integrates directional targeted liquid cargo release, shape morphing, and locomotion, which establishes the groundwork for target drug delivery in gastrointestinal tract applications.

</details>


### [60] [RoverDevKit: An open, physics-grounded tradespace toolkit for conceptual design of lunar micro-rovers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21755)
*Jon Reifschneider*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为RoverDevKit的开源分析评估工具，用于月球微型探测车的预阶段A设计。该工具结合了地形力学、质量、电力、热生存和行进性能的评估，在30毫秒内完成一次任务模拟，适用于多目标优化。研究发现，在不同的任务场景下，决定性设计因素会有所不同，并且在平滑表土范围-质量-坡度目标下，四轮刚性布局在整个建模的质量范围内占优。此外，六轮摇臂式布局仅在任务需要障碍导航时才进入帕累托最优集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对50公斤以下级别的月球微型探测车，在其设计初期（预阶段A）面临着移动性、能源、热控及质量等紧密关联的设计权衡问题。然而，目前面向这一快速增长领域的概念设计工具通常是专有的、基准测试不足或运行速度不足以支持优化过程。

Method: 作者开发了一个名为RoverDevKit的开放源代码分析评估器，它可以快速地（每次任务30毫秒）综合考虑地形力学、质量、电力、热生存以及行进距离等因素。使用NSGA-II算法生成不同任务场景下的帕累托前沿，以探索不同任务配置下关键设计因素的变化。

Result: 研究结果显示，在相同质量等级但不同任务背景下，制约设计的主要因素会发生变化：高纬度地区能量存储成为主要限制因素；高地松散表土上斜坡牵引力成为关键；而海面和平原边缘任务中，则是行进距离起主导作用。值得注意的是，对于平滑表土条件下追求最佳范围-质量-坡度表现的目标而言，四轮刚性布局在整个模型质量范围内均表现出优势。六轮摇臂布局只有当任务明确要求具备障碍物导航能力时才会出现在帕累托最优解集中。

Conclusion: 通过提供一个快速有效的开放式评估平台，RoverDevKit能够帮助设计师更好地理解并解决月球微型探测车早期设计阶段遇到的各种复杂权衡问题。此外，所有相关工具、数据验证材料及图表生成脚本均已公开发布。

Abstract: Pre-Phase-A design of lunar micro-rovers is dominated by tightly coupled mobility, power, thermal, and mass trades, yet conceptual-design tooling for the rapidly growing sub-50 kg class is typically proprietary, weakly benchmarked, or too slow to drive optimization. We contribute RoverDevKit, an open analytical evaluator coupling terramechanics, mass, power, thermal survival, and traverse that runs in 30ms per mission, fast enough to serve directly as a multi-objective optimizer's fitness function. Across mare, polar, highland, and crater-rim scenarios, NSGA-II Pareto fronts show that the binding design trade changes with mission profile within a single mass class: energy storage dominates at high latitude, slope traction on loose highland regolith, and traverse range on mare and crater-rim missions. Notably, rigid four-wheel layouts Pareto-dominate the full modeled mass range under smooth-regolith range-mass-slope objectives, contrary to the expectation that six-wheel architectures become optimal at heavier masses; six-wheel rocker-bogie layouts enter the Pareto set only once missions impose an obstacle-navigation requirement. The evaluator performance is benchmarked using both component and system checks: the terramechanics kernel matches measured single-wheel drawbar pull within the literature model-form band on two independent datasets, the bottom-up mass model predicts published in-class (5-50 kg) rover masses to 13.3% median absolute error, and a rediscovery check places real micro-rovers near the optimizer's fronts. Propagating the measured terramechanics error through the optimizer leaves the qualitative design rules unchanged. The tool, data, validation artifacts, and figure-generation scripts are released openly.

</details>


### [61] [A Novel Bio-Inspired Fish Robot with Tunable Stiffness via Particle Jamming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21771)
*Jack Stonecipher,Allen Gao,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过设计一种可快速调节硬度的仿生鱼机器人，探索了身体硬度可调对游泳性能的影响。实验结果表明，在不同摆动频率下，主动调整硬度对于保持高效和高速游泳至关重要。低频时较软的身体有助于提高速度并降低运输成本；高频时则相反，更硬的身体表现出更好的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探讨身体硬度调节如何影响游泳效率，以模仿鱼类在不同速度下游泳时通过改变身体柔韧性来实现高效移动的能力。

Method: 采用了一种灵感来源于生物的自由游动鱼形机器人，其具有可通过粒子堵塞技术快速调节硬度的身体。通过可视化振荡体的中线并在不同硬度条件下进行比较，以及使用运动跟踪系统测量速度和运输成本（CoT）来评估可调硬度身体对游泳性能的影响。

Result: 结果显示，在1-3Hz的拍打频率范围内，主动调整身体硬度对于维持高效的高速游泳非常重要。特别是在较低频率(1-1.5Hz)下，较软的身体能够最大化速度并最小化CoT；而在较高频率(2.5-3Hz)下，较硬的身体则能提供更高的速度和更低的运输成本。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过调整身体硬度可以作为适应性和高效推进的关键策略，适用于受生物启发的机器人游泳者。

Abstract: Fish achieve efficient swimming across varied speeds through active modulation of their body flexibility. To explore the effects of tunable stiffness on swimming performance, we present a bio-inspired freely swimming fish robot with a rapidly tunable particle-jamming body. This design enables rapid stiffness adjustments with negligible changes in shape or volume, achieving a 54% variation in flexural rigidity across vacuum pressures of 0 to -40 kPa. We visualize the midline of the oscillating body under both low- and high-stiffness conditions, and the comparison confirms that the body curvature varies with stiffness. We further experimentally evaluate the tunable stiffness body's effects on swimming performance using velocity and cost of transport (CoT) measurements obtained via a motion tracking system. Results show that active stiffness tuning is essential for sustaining efficient and high-speed swimming across beating frequencies of 1-3 Hz. At low frequencies (1-1.5 Hz), a softer body (0 kPa) maximizes velocity and minimizes CoT, whereas at high frequencies (2.5-3 Hz), a stiffer body (-40 kPa) delivers superior velocity and reduced transport cost. These findings highlight stiffness modulation as a key strategy for adaptive and efficient propulsion in bio-inspired robotic swimmers.

</details>


### [62] [Rotation-Aware Point-Cloud Embeddings for Vision-Based In-Hand Reorientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21788)
*Yashom Dighe,Karthik Dantu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种旋转感知的点云嵌入方法，其欧几里得潜在距离校准到对象方向之间的SO(3)测地线误差。此表示法将当前-目标比较转化为平滑的控制信号，使无模型的强化学习策略能够仅从当前和目标点云嵌入、本体感受和质心元数据中行动，而无需物体姿态、相对姿态、密集流或教师动作监督。实验表明，这种接口在保持性能的同时避免了结构化姿态或流输入的脆弱测试时计算，并且通用视觉点云预训练不足以进行此类当前-目标比较，因为它只保留了形状特征而丢弃了与任务相关状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决点云目标条件下的策略学习问题，即当前和目标点云之间存在不一致性和对应关系不稳定的问题，本文旨在开发一种新的点云嵌入方法，以提高基于点云的灵巧手内重定向任务的表现。

Method: 研究者们提出了一种新的旋转感知点云嵌入技术，通过确保欧几里得潜在空间中的距离反映SO(3)群上对象旋转之间的实际差异来实现。该方法允许使用无模型强化学习直接从原始感官数据（如点云）中学习策略，而不需要额外的姿态估计或者密集流特征。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法能够在没有额外结构化信息的情况下达到与特权状态和蒸馏基线相当的表现，同时避免了测试时对结构化姿态或流输入的依赖。此外，研究还指出，通用的视觉点云预训练对于解决特定于任务的当前-目标比较问题可能不够充分。

Conclusion: 通过改进点云表示本身而非依赖外部模块来编码旋转相关的几何信息，可以使点云目标成为灵巧手内重定向任务中更实用的选择。

Abstract: Point-cloud goals provide a direct way to specify dexterous in-hand reorientation: instead of defining an object-specific pose frame or estimating 6D pose at test time, the policy is given the desired 3D geometry of the object. Yet raw point-cloud goal conditioning is poorly conditioned for policy learning. Current and goal clouds are unordered, independently sampled, and often visibility-dependent, so their discrepancy entangles object rotation with permutation, resampling, and unstable correspondence structure. For this reason, prior point-cloud manipulation methods typically add structure outside the representation itself, such as explicit pose or relative-pose inputs, dense flow features, or distillation from privileged teachers. We close this gap by learning a rotation-aware point-cloud embedding whose Euclidean latent distance is calibrated to the SO(3) geodesic error between object orientations. The resulting representation turns current-goal comparison into a smooth control signal, allowing a model-free RL policy to act from current and goal point-cloud embeddings, proprioception, and centroid metadata, without object pose, relative pose, dense flow, or teacher-action supervision. In in-hand reorientation experiments, this interface matches privileged-state and distillation-based baselines while avoiding brittle test-time computation of structured pose or flow inputs. These results suggest that point-cloud goals become practical for this task when the representation, rather than an external module, encodes the task-relevant geometry of rotation. We also show evidence that generic visual point-cloud pretraining is insufficient for such a current-goal comparison because it discards the task-relevant state and preserves only shape features.

</details>


### [63] [THREAD: Trajectory Planning for Hybrid Rigid-Soft Manipulators with Environment-Aware Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21792)
*Shivani Kamtikar,Pranav Asthana,Naveen Kumar Uppalapati,Girish Krishnan,Girish Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了THREAD，一种基于扩散的轨迹规划器，专为混合刚性-柔性操作器设计，能够学习根据局部环境几何形状生成物理上可实现的骨架轨迹，并通过模拟训练实现了高达92.4%的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在狭窄空间中进行操控对于传统刚性机器人来说是一个基本挑战。虽然混合刚性-柔性操作器提供了希望，但它们面临着两个复杂的规划难题：一是在自由空间中可行的骨架形状在与环境接触时变得不可行；二是独立规划刚性和柔性部分忽视了它们之间的运动学耦合。

Method: 研究者们提出了THREAD，这是首个用于混合操作的基于扩散的轨迹规划器。它能根据局部环境几何结构学习生成物理上可实现的骨架轨迹。该方法利用受物理启发的损失函数来共同编码曲率、平滑度和碰撞约束，适用于刚性和柔性部分。

Result: 经过模拟训练后，THREAD达到了92.4%的任务成功率，相较于最强基线减少了5倍的碰撞次数。此外，它展示了跨实体的真实世界迁移能力，只需少量在线更新即可成功穿过直径仅为软段1.3倍的小孔径。

Conclusion: THREAD证明了基于扩散的方法可以有效地解决混合刚性-柔性操作器面临的规划挑战，提供了一种新的途径来克服狭窄空间内的操作困难。

Abstract: Manipulation in confined environments, such as threading a manipulator through narrow apertures, remains a fundamental challenge, especially for conventional rigid robots. Hybrid rigid-soft manipulators offer promise but face two compounding planning challenges: backbone shapes feasible in free space become infeasible under environmental contact, and planning rigid and soft segments independently ignores their kinematic coupling. We present THREAD, the first diffusion-based trajectory planner for hybrid manipulation, learning a generative prior over physically realizable backbone trajectories conditioned on local environment geometry, with physics-inspired losses encoding curvature, smoothness, and collision constraints jointly across both segments. Trained in simulation, THREAD achieves 92.4% task success with 5x fewer collisions than the strongest baseline. We show cross-embodiment real-world transfer with minimal online updates, successfully threading through apertures as small as 1.3x the soft segment diameter.

</details>


### [64] [SurGE: Surrogate Gradient-guided Evolution for Co-design of Legged Robots with Parallel Elasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21866)
*Yulun Zhuang,Yue Qin,Justin Lu,Zelin Shen,Yichen Wang,Sicheng He,Yanran Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SurGE的框架，通过可微分管道计算设计目标的代理梯度，并将其注入CMA-ES以优化有弹性元件的腿式机器人的协同设计。该方法相比传统的CMA-ES在减少跨种子标准差和提高种群集中度上表现出色，同时能够在硬件实验中有效降低设计目标值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带弹性元件的腿式机器人协同设计由于接触动力学和机构参与的不可微性而变得具有挑战性。为了克服这个问题并加速此类非可微协同设计问题的解决过程，本文提出了新的方法。

Method: SurGE框架使用了包括一个运动学单刚体(Kino-SRB)模型和一种设计感知控制策略在内的可微分流程来计算设计目标的代理梯度，并通过带有余弦退火步长衰减的均值偏移将这些梯度注入到CMA-ES算法中。

Result: 在具有单向平行弹簧的跳跃机器人4自由度设计空间内，SurGE实现了比传统CMA-ES低6倍的跨种子标准偏差以及18%更紧凑的人口集中度，同时达到了相同或更好的目标效果。此外，在2D设计子空间上的硬件实验表明，从手动调整的初始设计开始，SurGE能够将实际硬件的设计目标减少37.65%，并且仿真中的改进趋势一致地转移到了物理系统上。

Conclusion: SurGE框架展示了通过代理模型梯度加速解决腿式机器人中非可微协同设计问题的可能性，不仅提高了优化效率还保证了结果的一致性和可靠性。

Abstract: Co-design of legged robots with elastic elements is challenging due to the non-differentiability of contact dynamics and mechanism engagement. This paper presents SurGE, a framework that computes surrogate gradients of the design objective through a differentiable pipeline consisting of a kinodynamic single-rigid-body (Kino-SRB) model and a design-aware control policy, and injects them into CMA-ES via mean shift with cosine-annealed step decay. On a 4-DOF design space of a hopping robot with unidirectional parallel spring, SurGE achieves 6 times lower cross-seed standard deviation and 18% tighter population concentration compared to vanilla CMA-ES, while matching or improving the best objective. Hardware experiments on a 2D design subspace show that, starting from a hand-tuned initial design, SurGE reduces the design objective by 37.65% on hardware, with the improvement trend identified in simulation transferring consistently to the physical system. SurGE provides the potential to accelerate non-differentiable co-design problems in legged robots via surrogate model gradients.

</details>


### [65] [Predictive Gaze Is Preserved but Reorganized toward Monitoring during Robot-Mediated Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21920)
*Manuela Uliano,Silvia Fattorini,Marco Controzzi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现，即使在通过机器人执行任务时，人类的视觉运动控制仍然保持了其预测性，尽管注意力会更多地转移到机器人末端执行器和被操作物体上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索当人们通过机器人而不是自己的身体执行动作时，目标导向的眼动行为（一种基本的视觉运动控制）是否仍然保留其预期性和任务驱动性。

Method: 通过观察远程操作期间的目标导向眼动行为，来研究视觉运动控制如何适应改变的身体体验。

Result: 结果显示，即使在机器人介导的操作中，注视仍然与任务目标高度一致，保持了其预测作用；同时，远程操作系统地将视觉注意力重新分配到机器人末端执行器和被操作对象上，增加了在线监控。

Conclusion: 这表明，即使在身体体验改变的情况下，预测性注视也不会丢失，而是根据感官反馈和控制需求的变化进行重组。这些发现揭示了自然感觉运动耦合被打断时人类视觉运动系统的灵活性，并指出注视可以作为推断人机交互中行动意图的信息信号。

Abstract: Goal-directed eye movements are a fundamental component of visuomotor control, enabling humans to anticipate and guide their actions. Whether this anticipatory and task-driven behavior is preserved when actions are executed through a robot rather than through one's own body remains unclear. Here we address this question by investigating gaze behavior during goal-directed telemanipulation to determine how visuomotor control adapts to altered embodiment. Our findings show that gaze remains strongly aligned with task goals, preserving its predictive role even during robot-mediated manipulation. At the same time, teleoperation systematically redistributes visual attention toward the robotic end-effector and manipulated objects, increasing online monitoring. These findings show that predictive gaze is not lost under altered embodiment, but reorganized in response to changes in sensory feedback and control demands. More broadly, they reveal the flexibility of the human visuomotor system when the natural sensorimotor coupling is disrupted and identify gaze as an informative signal for inferring action intentions in human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [66] [CoRDE: Concept-Prior Routed Diffusion Experts for Structural Generalization in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21935)
*Haidong Huang,Xixin Zhao,Yaohua Zhou,Jiayu Song,Jiayi Zhang,Jun Ma,Haiyue Zhu,Xiaocong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CoRDE的结构引导变分蒸馏框架，旨在解决机器人模仿学习中单一架构在多任务和长时序场景下缺乏结构泛化能力的问题。通过从冻结的概念编码器中提取语义分布来指导可学习的软映射矩阵，并采用低秩适应技术以提高参数效率，从而减少了路由崩溃，实现了高质量的动作生成和增量学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型在捕捉机器人模仿学习中的多模态动作分布方面表现出色，但在多任务和长时序情境下，单一架构由于不同语义子阶段间的梯度冲突而缺乏结构泛化能力。此外，纯数据驱动的专家混合方法虽然引入了劳动分工机制，但经常导致路由崩溃；同时，完全实例化的专家会导致参数激增及高昂的扩展成本。为了解决这些问题，作者提出了一个新的框架——概念先验路由扩散专家（CoRDE）。

Method: CoRDE通过从一个固定的‘概念编码器’中抽取语义分布，利用一个可学习的软映射矩阵来指导变分后验责任的推断过程。该机制引入了一个熵控制的责任推断流程，在可靠的语义预测下鼓励确定性路由的同时保留随机扩散项以保持行为多样性。为了应对参数膨胀问题，CoRDE使用基于共享固定骨干网络上的低秩适应（LoRA）技术构建了一个参数高效的专家池。

Result: 理论分析表明，混合得分差异受到责任加权局部专家误差的限制，在低秩专家适应条件下支持高保真生成。实证评估证实，与现有基线相比，CoRDE系统地减少了路由崩溃现象，形成了稳健且语义对齐的专家分配方案，同时达到了更优的动作质量和增量学习效率。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的CoRDE框架有效地解决了传统方法在处理复杂多任务和长时序场景时面临的挑战，通过创新性的结构设计不仅提高了模型的行为多样性和生成质量，还显著增强了其在新任务上的学习效率。

Abstract: Diffusion models excel at capturing multi-modal action distributions in robot imitation learning. However, in multi-task and long-horizon scenarios, monolithic architectures lack structural generalization capabilities, suffering from gradient conflicts between distinct semantic sub-stages. While pure data-driven Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) methods introduce labor division, they frequently trigger routing collapse, and instantiating full-scale experts causes parameter explosion and high expansion costs. To address these issues, we propose Concept-prior Routed Diffusion Experts (CoRDE), a structure-guided variational distillation framework. CoRDE extracts semantic distributions from a frozen concept encoder to guide the variational posterior responsibility via a learnable soft mapping matrix. This mechanism introduces an entropy-controlled responsibility inference process that encourages confident routing under reliable semantic predictions while preserving the stochastic diffusion term for behavioral diversity. To overcome parameter inflation, CoRDE employs a parameter-efficient expert pool using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on a shared frozen backbone. Theoretical analysis shows that the mixture score discrepancy is bounded by responsibility-weighted local expert errors, supporting high-fidelity generation under low-rank expert adaptation. Empirical evaluations confirm that, compared to existing baselines, CoRDE systematically reduces routing collapse, forming robust, semantically aligned expert allocations while achieving superior action quality and incremental learning efficiency.

</details>


### [67] [RARM: Confidence-Gated Progress Reward Modeling for RL in Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22027)
*Pengzhi Yang,Xinyu Wang,Pengyu Jing,Kehan Wen,Yiduo Qu,Zhenhao Huang,Minghao Fu,Xin Liu,Yaheng Shen,Fan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为RARM的参考锚定奖励模型，该模型通过对比成功的单次演示来生成密集且进度感知的奖励。RARM在通用视频上进行一次性训练，无需特定于任务或机器人的数据，并且在长时任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在机器人操作中的应用经常受限于奖励设计，特别是在长时间的任务中：稀疏的成功奖励提供较弱的监督信号，而手工设计的密集奖励则既繁琐又难以跨任务泛化。基于进展的奖励模型提供了一个有希望的替代方案，但现有方法通常需要特定于任务的演示或进展标签，并且可能会给视觉上合理但实际上物理不正确的状态分配高奖励。

Method: 提出了一种名为参考锚定奖励模型（RARM）的方法，它作为一个轻量级的视觉比较器工作，能够将一次成功的演示转化为密集且进度敏感的奖励。RARM仅需在具有对比时间目标的一般用途视频上进行一次性训练，不需要任何特定于机器人的数据、特定于任务的奖励标签或针对每个任务的奖励工程。部署时，RARM会将执行过程片段与参考片段匹配起来，并且只奖励那些确定向前推进的情况，从而抑制可能导致误报奖励的不确定匹配。

Result: 在LIBERO和MetaWorld的9个模拟操作任务以及4个真实世界任务中，RARM在随后的RL训练中取得了最佳的整体成功率，尤其在像布料折叠这样的长时间任务上表现出了显著的优势，在这些任务中不可靠的进度估计特别有害。

Conclusion: RARM为解决长时间任务中强化学习的奖励设计问题提供了有效途径，通过利用单次成功演示生成密集型进度奖励，减少了对人工设计奖励的需求，并提高了机器人在多样化任务上的性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for robot manipulation is often bottlenecked by reward design, especially in long-horizon tasks: sparse success rewards provide weak supervision, while hand-crafted dense rewards are tedious to design and generalize poorly across tasks. Progress-based reward models offer a promising alternative by estimating how far an observation has advanced toward task completion, but existing approaches often require task-specific demonstrations or progress labels, and can assign high rewards to visually plausible but physically incorrect states. We introduce the Reference-Anchored Reward Model (RARM), a lightweight visual comparator that converts a single successful demonstration into a dense, progress-aware reward. RARM is trained once on general-purpose videos with a contrastive temporal objective, requiring no robot-specific data, task-specific reward labels, or per-task reward engineering. At deployment, RARM matches rollout clips to reference clips and rewards only confident forward progress, suppressing uncertain matches that may otherwise produce false-positive rewards. Across 9 simulated manipulation tasks from LIBERO and MetaWorld and 4 real-world tasks, RARM achieves the best overall success rates in subsequent RL training, with particularly large gains on long-horizon tasks such as cloth folding, where unreliable progress estimates are especially harmful.

</details>


### [68] [Deep RL- Tuned Mo del-Free Adaptive Control for Lower-Limb Exoskeletons During Sit-to-Stand Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22040)
*Ranjeet Kumbhar,Appaso M. Gadade,Rajmeet Singh,Ashish Singla,Ravinder Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于智能无模型自适应反步控制策略的双侧下肢外骨骼控制器，用于在坐立转换动作中提供辅助。该控制器使用超局部二阶模型并结合高斯径向基函数神经网络在线估计未知动态，并通过TD3强化学习代理自适应调整控制增益。仿真结果表明，所提出的控制器相比现有技术显著降低了关节角度的跟踪误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 坐立转换（STS）动作对老年人来说是一个关节负荷需求较高的过程，因此是下肢外骨骼辅助的一个重要目标。然而，由于复杂的时间变化人机交互动力学以及个体间差异性，使得基于模型的控制方法难以实际应用。

Method: 提出了一种新的无模型自适应反步控制器设计，它利用了超局部二阶模型来避免显式的系统辨识问题；同时采用高斯RBF神经网络在线估计未建模的动力学部分。为了提高相位意识下的追踪性能，还集成了一个TD3强化学习算法作为监督增益调度器，在STS动作的不同阶段自适应地调节控制器增益。

Result: 通过MATLAB/Simulink和Simscape Multibody联合仿真评估了所提控制器的表现，并与PID、MFAC、LQR及SMC等先进控制器进行了对比。结果显示，新控制器在所有关节上实现了最低平均RMSE 0.078度的成绩，分别比其他四种控制器提高了60.2%、54.4%、48.7%和42.6%。此外，加入TD3后进一步减少了髋、膝、踝三个关节处的跟踪误差达35%、33%和79%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，所提出的智能无模型自适应反步控制策略结合TD3强化学习算法能够有效且鲁棒地控制辅助外骨骼执行STS动作，为老年人或需要帮助的人群提供了更精确的支持。

Abstract: Sit-to-stand (STS) transitions impose significant joint-loading demands on elderly individuals, making them a primary target for lower-limb exoskeleton assistance. However, accurate trajectory tracking during STS is challenging due to complex, time-varying human exoskeleton interaction dynamics and inter-subject variability that render model-based control approaches difficult to apply in practice. This paper presents an intelligent model free adaptive backstepping control strategy for a bilateral lower-limb exoskeleton during STS motion. The proposed controller design uses an ultra-local second-order model to avoid explicit system identification, while a Gaussian radial basis function (RBF) neural network estimates the unknown lumped dynamics online. To further improve phase-aware tracking performance, a Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) reinforcement learning agent is integrated as a supervisory gain scheduler that adaptively adjusts controller gains across the distinct phases of STS motion. The proposed controller is evaluated through co-simulation in MATLAB/Simulink and Simscape Multibody using OpenSim-derived reference trajectories and benchmarked against state-of-the-art controllers. Results demonstrate that the proposed controller achieves the lowest average RMSE of 0.078 degree across all joints, representing improvements of 60.2%, 54.4%, 48.7%, and 42.6% over proportional integral derivative (PID), model-free adaptive control (MFAC), linear quadratic regulator (LQR), and sliding-mode control (SMC), respectively. TD3 integration further reduces tracking error by 35%, 33%, and 79% at the hip, knee, and ankle joints compared to the standalone RBF-MFAC baseline. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed controller design for assistive exoskeleton control during STS transitions.

</details>


### [69] [A Multimodal Tiltwing Framework for Bioinspired Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22046)
*Krispin C. V. Broers,Sophie F. Armanini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种可适应的倾斜翼框架，使仿生飞行机器人能够在悬停、高速定向飞行和节能滑翔之间切换。通过独立推进单元实现推力矢量控制，结合了被动翼旋转机制以增强设计自由度，并通过无接触前缘跟踪传感器提供精确反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无尾拍翼微型飞行器虽然模仿了蜂鸟令人印象深刻的飞行性能，但它们的设计仍然受限，且用途单一，飞行范围有限，续航能力差。

Method: 开发了一种混合式Scotch-yoke（苏格兰轭）拍打机制，确保对称运动的同时允许任意宽的拍打角度，以利用升力增强的拍打效果。此外，还实现了一个被动翼旋转机制，与双翼推力矢量方法相结合，提供了前所未有的翼设计自由度。一个非接触式的前缘追踪传感器被用来提供关于翅膀方向的准确反馈，在滑翔模式下还能控制二面角。进行了广泛的测试，包括使用六轴力/扭矩传感器对推进单元进行测试，以优化传动效率和被动翼俯仰机制的表现。

Result: 在全油门状态下，单个翼（拍打幅度为188°）产生的平均升力为21.1 gf，对于一个小至3.1 g的1S BLDC电机而言表现良好。全面覆盖大角度倾斜能力范围的额外测试显示了有效的推力矢量控制架构，生成的力矩响应曲线呈现出线性和对称性。

Conclusion: 所提出的倾斜翼框架及其实现技术为提高生物启发式空中机器人的飞行性能和能效提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Tailless flapping-wing micro-aerial vehicles (FWMAVs) mimic the impressive flight performance of hummingbirds, utilising unsteady aerodynamic effects. However, existing designs are still limited and purpose-built with a restricted flight envelope and poor endurance. We therefore propose an adaptable tiltwing framework enabling bioinspired aerial robots to switch between hovering flight, high-speed directional flight, and energy-efficient gliding flight. The proposed framework utilises thrust vectoring with a wide actuation range via two fully independent propulsion units, each flapping a single wing, for effective control and enhanced manoeuvrability. For this, we developed a hybrid Scotch-yoke-based flapping mechanism that ensures a symmetric motion profile with a modular design guaranteeing an arbitrarily wide flapping angle to exploit the lift-enhancing clap-and-fling effect. Additionally, we implemented a passive wing-rotation mechanism, which, in combination with our dual-wing thrust-vectoring approach, allows unprecedented wing-design freedom, unlocking potential for precise optimisation. A contactless leading-edge tracking sensor provides accurate feedback on the wing's orientation and, in the gliding mode, enables dihedral-angle control, augmenting the active wing-pitch control. Extensive testing of a propulsion unit was conducted with a six-axis force/torque sensor, demonstrating the flapping mechanism's performance while optimising transmission efficiency and the passive wing-pitch mechanism. At full throttle, the average lift force generated by a single wing, flapping with a 188° amplitude, was 21.1 gf for a small 3.1 g 1S BLDC motor. Additional tests covering the full range of the wide-angle tilting capability showed an effective thrust-vectoring control architecture with a linear and symmetric response curve of the moments generated.

</details>


### [70] [GeoFlow-SLAM++: A Robust Multi-Camera Visual-Inertial SLAM System with Relocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22051)
*Wei Fen,Tingyang Xiao,Liu Liu,Xiaolin Zhou,Zhizhong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoFlow-SLAM++ is a multi-camera visual-inertial SLAM system that enhances robustness and accuracy in various scenarios, including appearance-challenging ones, through the use of a unified body-centric formulation, two interchangeable visual front-ends (ORB and NN-Feature), and an optional cross-view-consistent pseudo-depth prediction mechanism.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation for this paper is to overcome the limitations of monocular and RGB-D visual-inertial SLAM systems, such as limited field of view, sensor-specific failure modes, and unreliable relocalization across sessions.
